# Whereas the Veterinary Council of India in pursuance of sub section (1) of Section 23 of Indian Veterinary Council Act, 1984 (52 of 1984) is required to maintain a register known as Indian Veterinary

VETERINARY COUNCIL OF INDIA NOTIFICATION New Delhi, the 9th February, 2026 F. No. 10-7/2025-VCI.—Whereas the Veterinary Council of India in pursuance of sub-section

(1) of Section 23 of Indian Veterinary Council Act, 1984 (52 of 1984) is required to maintain a register known as Indian Veterinary Practitioners Register which shall contain the names of all persons who possess the recognised veterinary qualification and who are for the time being enrolled on State/UT Veterinary Council of the State to which this Act extends.

Now, in exercise of powers conferred by sub-section (2) of Section 23 of the Indian Veterinary Council Act, 1984 (52 of 1984) the Secretary, Veterinary Council of India, directs that Supplement to Indian Veterinary Practitioners Register, 2025 shall be published in the Gazette of India for general information.

Dr. R. G. BAMBAL, Secy., VCI (Addl.Charge)
